Furthermore, the local state stores a database of blocks and votes,
an inbox for unread messages, and the current finalized chain.

<pre class="Agda"><a id="2487" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="LocalState"></a><a id="2494" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2494" class="Record">LocalState</a> <a id="2505" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2507" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">Type</a> <a id="2512" class="Keyword">where</a>
  <a id="2520" class="Keyword">constructor</a> <a id="⦅_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_⦆"></a><a id="2532" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2532" class="InductiveConstructor Operator">⦅_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_,_⦆</a>
  <a id="2558" class="Keyword">field</a>
    <a id="LocalState.r-vote"></a><a id="2568" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2568" class="Field">r-vote</a>    <a id="2578" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2580" href="Jolteon.Base.html#101" class="Function">Round</a>     <a id="2590" class="Comment">-- highest voted round</a>
    <a id="2617" class="Comment">-- r-lock    : Round     -- highest locked round</a>
    <a id="LocalState.r-cur"></a><a id="2670" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2670" class="Field">r-cur</a>     <a id="2680" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2682" href="Jolteon.Base.html#101" class="Function">Round</a>     <a id="2692" class="Comment">-- current round</a>
    <a id="LocalState.qc-high"></a><a id="2713" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2713" class="Field">qc-high</a>   <a id="2723" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2725" href="Jolteon.Block.html#1905" class="Function">QC</a>        <a id="2735" class="Comment">-- highest quorum certificate</a>
    <a id="LocalState.tc-last"></a><a id="2769" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2769" class="Field">tc-last</a>   <a id="2779" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2781" href="Agda.Builtin.Maybe.html#135" class="Datatype">Maybe</a> <a id="2787" href="Jolteon.Block.html#4423" class="Record">TC</a>  <a id="2791" class="Comment">-- last round timeout certificate</a>

    <a id="LocalState.phase"></a><a id="2830" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2830" class="Field">phase</a>      <a id="2841" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2843" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#402" class="Datatype">Phase</a>     <a id="2853" class="Comment">-- current phase</a>
    <a id="LocalState.db"></a><a id="2874" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2874" class="Field">db</a>         <a id="2885" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2887" href="Jolteon.Message.html#928" class="Function">Messages</a>  <a id="2897" class="Comment">-- list of processed messages</a>
    <a id="LocalState.inbox"></a><a id="2931" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2931" class="Field">inbox</a>      <a id="2942" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2944" href="Jolteon.Message.html#928" class="Function">Messages</a>  <a id="2954" class="Comment">-- messages received, but not yet processed</a>
    <a id="LocalState.finalChain"></a><a id="3002" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3002" class="Field">finalChain</a> <a id="3013" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3015" href="Jolteon.Block.html#9128" class="Function">Chain</a>     <a id="3025" class="Comment">-- the most recent committed chain</a>

    <a id="LocalState.timer"></a><a id="3065" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3065" class="Field">timer</a>      <a id="3076" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3078" href="Agda.Builtin.Maybe.html#135" class="Datatype">Maybe</a> <a id="3084" href="Jolteon.Base.html#111" class="Function">Time</a> <a id="3089" class="Comment">-- (optional) timer to fire</a>

The initial local state is the empty one:
<pre class="Agda"><a id="initLocalState"></a><a id="3506" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3506" class="Function">initLocalState</a> <a id="3521" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="3523" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2494" class="Record">LocalState</a>

  <a id="3560" class="Symbol">{</a> <a id="3562" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2568" class="Field">r-vote</a> <a id="3569" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3571" class="Number">0</a>
  <a id="3575" class="Comment">-- ; r-lock = 0</a>
  <a id="3593" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3595" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2670" class="Field">r-cur</a> <a id="3601" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3603" class="Number">1</a>
  <a id="3607" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3609" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2713" class="Field">qc-high</a> <a id="3617" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3619" href="Jolteon.Block.html#9577" class="Function">qc₀</a>
  <a id="3625" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3627" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2769" class="Field">tc-last</a> <a id="3635" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3637" href="Agda.Builtin.Maybe.html#194" class="InductiveConstructor">nothing</a>
  <a id="3647" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3649" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2830" class="Field">phase</a> <a id="3655" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3657" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#423" class="InductiveConstructor">EnteringRound</a>
  <a id="3673" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3675" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2874" class="Field">db</a> <a id="3678" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3680" href="Agda.Builtin.List.html#184" class="InductiveConstructor">[]</a>
  <a id="3685" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3687" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#2931" class="Field">inbox</a> <a id="3693" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3695" href="Agda.Builtin.List.html#184" class="InductiveConstructor">[]</a>
  <a id="3700" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3702" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3002" class="Field">finalChain</a> <a id="3713" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3715" href="Agda.Builtin.List.html#184" class="InductiveConstructor">[]</a>
  <a id="3720" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3722" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3065" class="Field">timer</a> <a id="3728" class="Symbol">=</a>  <a id="3731" href="Agda.Builtin.Maybe.html#194" class="InductiveConstructor">nothing</a>
  <a id="3741" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3743" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3121" class="Field">timedOut?</a> <a id="3753" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3755" href="Agda.Builtin.Bool.html#192" class="InductiveConstructor">false</a>
  <a id="3763" class="Symbol">;</a> <a id="3765" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#3181" class="Field">roundAdvanced?</a> <a id="3780" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="3782" href="Agda.Builtin.Bool.html#198" class="InductiveConstructor">true</a>
  <a id="3789" class="Symbol">}</a>
</pre><!--

### Block Membership

<pre class="Agda"><a id="5679" class="Comment">{-
data _-block-∈-_ : Block → Messages → Type where
  initialBlock :
    b ∙blockId ≡ genesisId
    ──────────────────────
    b -block-∈- ms

  proposedBlock :
    b ∈ allBlocks ms
    ────────────────
    b -block-∈- ms

  -- In the paper there&#39;s no other way of learning about a block.
  -- inQC :
  --     ∙ Any (qc qc∈-Message_) ms
  --     ∙ qc ∙blockId ≡ b ∙blockId
  --     ───────────────────────────
  --     b block-∈ ms

instance Has∈-Block = Has∈ Block ∋ λ where ._∙∈_ → _-block-∈-_
-}</a>

<a id="6179" class="Keyword">instance</a> <a id="Has∈-Block"></a><a id="6188" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#6188" class="Function">Has∈-Block</a> <a id="6199" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="6201" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#5321" class="Record">Has∈</a> <a id="6206" href="Jolteon.Block.html#7428" class="Record">Block</a> <a id="6212" href="Function.Base.html#4486" class="Function Operator">∋</a> <a id="6214" class="Symbol">λ</a> <a id="6216" class="Keyword">where</a> <a id="6222" class="Symbol">.</a><a id="6223" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#5359" class="Field Operator">_∙∈_</a> <a id="6228" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#6228" class="Bound">b</a> <a id="6230" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#6230" class="Bound">ms</a> <a id="6233" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="6235" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#6228" class="Bound">b</a> <a id="6237" href="Data.List.Membership.Setoid.html#925" class="Function Operator">∈</a> <a id="6239" href="Jolteon.Message.html#2797" class="Function">allBlocks</a> <a id="6249" href="Jolteon.Local.State.html#6230" class="Bound">ms</a>
</pre>
We define different Predicates on chains wrt to lists of messages.

### Chain membership

The following predicate indicates that the chain has been "seen"
(i. e. it is in the database).

A block is certified if there exists a qc for that block.
